WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: Q3 Labour Market Surveys (%)
Nov 4, Unemployment Rate, Last: 5.6, Westpac f/c: 5.5
LCI Private Sector, Last: 0.7, Westpac f/c: 0.5
We expect the unemployment rate to edge down to 5.5%
for the September quarter. Job numbers have been gaining
momentum in recent months, and now look to be enough to

outstrip population growth. However, the existing slack in the
labour market is likely to be keeping wage growth subdued,
despite cost-of-living pressures on workers. Our forecasts are
a little more positive overall than what the RBNZ assumed in
its September MPS forecasts.
US: Oct Employment Report
Nov 6, Payrolls, Last: 29k, Market f/c: 55k, WBC f/c: 75k
Nov 6, U/E rate, Last: 4.2%, Market f/c: 4.2%, WBC f/c: 4.3%
Nonfarm payrolls have shown considerable volatility of late,

July’s -10k followed by a 133k gain in August then a 29k
increase in September. However, the 3-month average of 51k
is likely a good guide on underlying momentum. In October, we
look for a 75k gain, although we expect some of that increase
to be offset by a modest reduction to the prior two months,
keeping the 3-month average around 50k.
While the participation rate has lifted in recent months, it
remains well below the average of 2025. Month-to-month, a

change either way is not guaranteed, but it seems likely that
the participation rate will trend higher in coming months. We
therefore expect the unemployment rate to edge up.

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

### Page 4

4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1810 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1710 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 314: Cliff Notes_ cost of living front and centre (2026-11-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ cost of living front and centre (2026-11-24).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-24
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
09:49 November 13 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment Index fell 2.7% to 78.2 in November, its weakest
reading since late-2022 and a level historically associated with periods of severe economic stress.
Renewed cost-of-living and interest rate pressures were front and centre following another lift in

inflation, including sharply higher petrol prices, and the RBA’s November rate increase. Mortgage
holders are feeling the squeeze most acutely, with sentiment across this group falling to a record low.

### Page 2

Consumers have become noticeably more downbeat on the near-term economic outlook – the relevant
sub-index dropping 10.2% to be 22% below its long-run average. Job-loss fears also continue to edge
up.
Businesses are similarly troubled. The latest NAB business survey showed confidence slipped further
to –15 in October as conditions held near their weakest level since the pandemic. Of concern for the
outlook, forward orders fell to –12, also the weakest read since the pandemic, and capital expenditure

intentions deteriorated. Purchase costs continue to grow materially faster than selling prices, further
compressing already tight margins.
Housing provides a clear example of how caution and tighter financial conditions can jolt activity. The
value of new dwelling finance approvals plunged 15% in Q3, the largest quarterly decline in the series’
history, taking approvals 25% lower year-to-date. Investor lending bore the brunt of the adjustment,
falling 30% over the quarter as higher rates combined with the housing tax changes announced in May.

Owner-occupier lending also weakened, however, with first-home buyers proving particularly sensitive
to the deterioration in affordability and market conditions.
Taken together, the data points to a further loss of momentum across the domestic economy,
consistent with Westpac’s expectation for subdued growth and concern over downside activity risks.
Meanwhile, in the US, October’s headline CPI met our expectations but at 0.4% was a touch above the

consensus view. Energy and food both contributed meaningfully to the result, but core inflation was
also marginally stronger than forecast, +0.3%. Core goods inflation has accelerated over the past four
months to average a gain of 0.2% as businesses look to pass tariffs, tech and energy input cost
inflation through. Shelter meanwhile continued to rise at an above-average pace, gaining 0.3% in
October.
The market impact of the October CPI report was accentuated by hawkish remarks from Fed Governor

Waller prior to the release. He expressed confidence in economic activity and the labour market while
showing significant concern over the strength and persistence of inflation, focusing on the passthrough
of energy and technology input inflation. His conclusion for policy was clear: "if, as I expect, the
monthly rate of inflation improves in October, I am leaning toward supporting an increase in our policy
rate of 25 basis points at the FOMC's December meeting"; but "if the October CPI inflation data out

later today are similar to the past two monthly readings—with core CPI inflation around 0.3 percent or
even higher—then a stronger policy move may be needed".
Regional Fed Presidents Goolsbee, Logan and Hammack also kept the focus on inflation risks and
stated or implied the FOMC needs to act. Market participants have responded to this week’s updates,
pricing a 25bp hike in for the December and January meetings, and a cumulative 3.5 hikes by
September. We believe this pricing overstates the need for policy tightening given core inflation ex

shelter remains relatively benign at 2.4% on a six-month annualised basis and the labour market is
balanced; but it will take time for price concerns to subside.
While we agree that the US economy is strong in aggregate, US households are under pressure from
the cost of living, and their investment decision making is constrained by borrowing costs. Highlighting

### Page 3

Related articles
this, US existing home sales fell 5.5% in October after a downwardly revised 1.0% gain in September,
leaving the level of sales below the cycle lows of 2009 and 2010. Our November Market Outlook will
provide a full update on our expectations for the US and the world when released on Westpac IQ this
afternoon.

## Document 345: FX Daily_ Commodity complex is having a moment

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Commodity complex is having a moment.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4

### Page 1

The dollar is bid across the board as a strong US economy and
above-target inﬂation keeps expectations of another 75bp in
Fed tightening alive. At the same time, commodity
currencies are starting to underperform as industrial metals
and energy take a leg lower. Investors are yet to lean into
another decent leg higher in the dollar, but that is the risk

Chris Turner, Francesco Pesole and Frantisek Taborsky
Domestic politics, dollar strength and falling commodities weigh on the
Brazilian real
Please see our 2027 FX Outlook for all the key themes driving
FX trends next year.
USD: Topside pressure building
We have been discussing it for more than a week now, but those
comments from the Fed's Chris Waller last Tuesday really focused
minds on an extended Fed tightening cycle. Since then, price data
has come in hot, while US activity data is also performing well. More

### Page 2

insights into the US economy will come from tonight's release of
the Fed's Beige Book ahead of the next FOMC meeting on 9
December. So far, the 15% correction in the Nasdaq has yet to really
dent those Fed tightening expectations. And tech stocks might
receive some support today from Nvidia's strong third-quarter
earnings release last night and today's Anthropic's $90bn IPO set
to price towards the high end of the $70-78 per share range.
Aside from the strong US economy and the Fed story, we are also

seeing some underperformance of commodity currencies. Here,
industrial metals are under pressure – especially iron ore – and even
copper is starting to turn lower. Our Commodity Strategist, Ewa
Manthey, discusses the prospect for industrial metals in the Latam
section of our 2027 FX Outlook. There seem to be both demand and
supply trends at work here, but ongoing pressure on industrial
metals usually spells trouble for the Australian dollar, the South

African rand and adds to the already weakened position of the
Brazilian real. With central banks looking to slow, not accelerate,
aggregate demand, it looks as though these trends could have a
little further to run.
Price action suggests investors, already long the dollar, are not yet
ready to chase the dollar much higher. But it is hard to stand in the
way of a DXY advance to the next resistance area around 104.60.
US housing data today looks unlikely to move markets.

Chris Turner
EUR: Dollar story dominates
Despite some seemingly better news out of France yesterday,
EUR/USD continues to trade below 1.10 as the dollar story
dominates. The FX options market suggests investors are yet to
lean into much further EUR/USD downside from here. For example,
one-month EUR/USD traded volatility has not moved much, while
the price of a one-month risk reversal – the price of a euro put over
a euro call option – remains steady near 0.5%. That probably

reﬂects a market already short EUR/USD and covering, rather than
opening new positions below 1.10.

Chris Turner
GBP: Further relative strength
EUR/GBP has corrected a little higher this European morning as the
latest services inﬂation data for October came in slightly below

expectations at 3.7% year-on-year. We doubt that is enough to
trigger a wholesale re-pricing of the UK money market curve,
although lower energy prices may be playing a greater role here
today.
In our 2027 FX Outlook, we have a 0.83 forecast for EUR/GBP at the
end of this year. A more hawkish Bank of England relative to the ECB
over the coming months and PM Andy Burnham's eﬀorts to get
closer to Europe are two factors which should keep EUR/GBP oﬀered.

This pair does exhibit very low realised volatility, and therefore the
150bp pick-up in GBP over EUR deposit rates is meaningful support
for sterling.
Chris Turner
BRL: Waiting on a new ﬁnance minister
The Brazilian real remains fragile as domestic politics, a strong
dollar and high US rates, plus now lower commodity prices all
weigh. The real looks unlikely to receive much of a reprieve from the
external environment anytime soon, but President Lula's choice of a

ﬁnance minister will have a big say in whether the domestic story
exacerbates or reverses the recent heavy losses in the real. On
Monday, a report that the left-leaning Aloizio Mercadante was
being considered for the position hit the real.
Given all the concerns over the ﬁscal trajectory in Brazil, the choice
of ﬁnance minister will be a key signal to the markets of President
Lula's agenda. Were a name like Geraldo Alckmin to emerge as a

WEEKLY AGGREGATES
ITEM DEFINITIONS AND METHODOLOGY
3© 2026 LSEG.  All marks herein are used under licence. TRPR_82222_875
General:
All aggregates are bottom-up aggregates calculated from analysts' consensus forecasts unless otherwise noted. Bottom-up
aggregates are weighted calculations using the I/B/E/S data from LSEG share class monitored or the official index weight of
the index-provider. Top-down forecasts are compilations of strategists' estimates. I/B/E/S universe calculations are based on

all companies within a given market monitored by LSEG.
Benchmark index calculations, including those for official local market indices, the S&P 500 Index, and the FTSE World Equity
Indices are calculated using official index constituents and weightings. All index composites are representative of current
index constituents. Index constituents and weightings are updated monthly during options expiration (the Thursday before the
third Friday of every month) for every index except the S&P 500, the constituents of which are current as of the date of

calculation.
PEIGBY refers to the Price Earnings Inverse Government Bond Yield ratio, which measures equity valuation with respect to
the inverse-yield on long-term government bonds (now known as the 'Fed' model). All PEIGBY ratios are I/B/E/S universe-
specific except for the US ratio, where it is based on the S&P 500 Index.
Methodology:
For US and selected Canadian aggregates, calendar-year aggregation is performed using the sum of four quarters' estimates

and a quarterly pro-ration is applied to non-December year end companies' quarterly EPS estimates. Outside of the US and
Canada, all company-level data are calendarized prior to aggregation using the Compustat method of calendarization.
Aggregation is performed using the annual consensus EPS estimate and a May roll-back is applied to December year-end
markets. Aggregates for Japan, where most companies report in March, use an August rollback and a November rollback is

used for Australia and New Zealand, where the fiscal year-end for most companies is in June.
All 12-month-forward data for ex-US markets are calculated using a pro-rata weighting to annual EPS forecasts. The
weightings applied are a daily calibration out of 365 days relative to each constituent's individual fiscal year-end and the
current date.
All non-surprise data, including aggregate forecast earnings, forecast growth, and forward-looking valuation calculations are

weighted according to the I/B/E/S share class monitored for I/B/E/S universes or the weightings assigned to each constituent
in a given index. Aggregated earnings calculations are the equivalent of net profit aggregates, as they are derived from the
EPS forecasts aggregated to the net income level by the appropriate share weighting.
Earnings Aggregation:
For 12-month-forward earnings, the 12-month-forward EPS is used in place of the calendarized EPS in the formulas below.

I/B/E/S universe EPS Aggregate = Index EPS Aggregate =
Growth Calculations:
All growth calculations are the weighted year over year growth in earnings for the calendarized fiscal year period. The
formulas below use index shares for index growth calculations.
Calendarized Earnings Growth = 12-month-forward Growth =
Valuation:
All valuation calculations are the weighted-average price-earnings ratio for the fiscal period in question. The formulas below

use index shares for index P/E calculations.
Calendarized P/E Aggregate = 12-month-forward P/E =
Revisions Momentum:
Unless otherwise indicated, analysts' revisions momentum is a simple aggregation of the total number of estimates raised
divided by the total number of estimates lowered. Thus, any revisions ratio less than 1 indicates negative revisions
momentum while a ratio greater than 1 indicates positive revisions momentum, with 1 marking parity.

The Global Aggregates from LSEG product contains annual aggregates for universes included in this publication, including
supplemental aggregate data of over 100 items as well as history dating back to 1985 for North America and to 1987 for other
markets. To learn more about this product, contact your LSEG sales representative.
FTSE and the FTSE World Equity Indices are owned by FTSE International Limited.

Soff (data centers), Daniel Knauff (software), George Hill (healthcare), Omotayo 
Okusanya (REITs), Peter Abramowitz (office REITs), Mark DeVries (consumer and 
specialty finance), Nicole DeBlase (MI/EE), Corinne Blanchard (clean tech), 
Richa Harnain (transports), and Scott Deuschle (aerospace and defense).  
With the 2026 US midterm elections approaching, we look at key investor 
debates surrounding the event and legislative priorities of the next Congress. 
Despite the uncertainty that midterms can bring, the market appears fairly 
confident on expectations for the overall results. While a surprise Republican-
majority victory in both chambers is still possible, consensus is converging 
around current polling indicating Democrats are likely to flip the seats needed for 
a House majority and may win a Senate majority. Historically, an incumbent 
president has lost seats in 20 of the last 22 midterms. Investor expectations are 
in line with this. Polymarket, for example, is currently above 90% for Democrats 
gaining control of the House and above 60% for Democrats gaining control of the 
Senate.  
With this set of expectations, key investor debates instead focus on the 
legislative agenda for hot-ticket policy issues. Namely, these hinge on AI and 
data centers, affordability (affecting healthcare, housing, and education loans), 
and economic and fiscal policy. Debates also surround possible influence of the 
new Congress on the war in Iran and, by extension, energy prices. In this report, 
we cut through the noise on what the next Congress could reasonably advance 
from these agendas, what the impact would be on affected sectors, and how to 
address market uncertainty – and perceived certainty – into the elections. 
Historically, midterm elections have proven to be a check on administration 
policies. The number of seats lost in the midterms by the incumbent party is tied 
to the president’s approval rating going into midterms, which in turn tends to 
align with consumer confidence. President Trump’s ~38% approval rating, at a 
record low for his tenure, would suggest a little over a 45-seat loss for the 
Republican Party (in the House and Senate combined). That said, polling in the 
Senate races that will determine control of the chamber largely remains within 
the margin of error, and in 2024, Trump won in at least five of the seven core toss-
up states. Thus, the Senate remains a much closer call than the House race.

Whatever the outcome of the midterms, the legislative agenda will 
still face constraints from the White House. It is all but impossible for either party 
to gain a veto-proof majority in either chamber, minimizing the likelihood of a 
large shift in fiscal or regulatory policy. The most immediately obvious impact of 
Democrats taking the Senate would be the ability to slow or block executive 
branch appointees, including at the Federal Reserve Board of Governors or the 
Supreme Court, should any members step down before their terms end.

### Page 3

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 3

Without garnering a two-thirds majority in both chambers, Democrats lack a clear 
path to unwind the 2025 tax cuts or enact AI-related regulatory legislation – at 
least, not without President Trump’s explicit approval. With respect to the near-
term fiscal policy outlook, the most important period for investors may be the 
“lame duck” session, the period after the November 3 election and before the new 
Congress convenes on January 3. Recall that current federal government funding 
expires on December 11, and the Trump administration is asking for a nearly 60% 
increase in defense spending, the largest since the Korean War. In addition, the 
debt limit will become binding again around the middle of next year. In short, the 
actions during the lame duck session could determine whether the new Congress 
is seated amid a government shutdown, a contentious battle over the federal 
budget, and the debt limit looming in the background.  
In terms of the market reaction to midterm elections, the risk-reward profile has 
typically been favorable, with 21 of the last 23 midterm years seeing positive 
returns in the fourth quarter, with an average gain of 7% in the S&P. The gains are 
notable even after adjusting for typical positive year-end seasonality.

Figure 3: S&P Returns and Midterms

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP

It would not be unusual for the market to go sideways as investors hedge 
potential volatility and uncertainty, as we have seen previously ahead of other 
well-telegraphed pivotal events. This has historically been followed by a rally as 
the results became clear and uncertainty dissipated, regardless of the result 
itself. In the current context, if election polls and prediction market probabilities 
continue being lopsided, the market may not treat the election as an uncertain 
risk event going in. Uncertainty could still ramp up after, and the typical midterm 
election rally may be pushed out. It is notable that the one prominent exception 
to the midterm rallies was in 2018, when the market sold off sharply after the 
elections as uncertainty spiked around trade war 1.0 as well as Fed policy.

### Page 4

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 4

We note that the midterm elections also coincide with the third quarter earnings 
season, during which we expect companies to deliver growth of 34%. For next 
year, we expect growth to be a still-robust 17% with risks to the upside. 
Eventually, earnings tend to be the anchor for equities and have been for several 
years despite numerous domestic political as well as geopolitical shocks. 
Across sectors, most have rallied around past midterms in keeping with the 
market, but there isn’t a consistent pattern of out- or underperformance. This 
suggests that for the initial conditions for each sector, specific policies and 
positioning played a bigger role. For that reason, we turn to a closer analysis of 
key legislative and policy debates impacting exposed sectors.

### Page 5

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 5

AI and Data Centers  
AI and data centers have become a campaign talking point for candidates from 
both the Democratic and Republican parties. This has spurred investor questions 
on whether to expect a national regulatory framework constraining data center 
buildouts. Some of the headlines surrounding this debate overestimate what 
Congress can accomplish through national legislation compared with what is 
possible at a state level, particularly during a presidential administration 
supportive of AI and data center acceleration. Still, the industry is responding to 
a political backdrop that is increasingly scrutinizing AI and data centers.  
The current administration has prioritized advancing AI and digital infrastructure 
development. The White House’s AI Action Plan, released July 2025, included a 
set of executive orders supporting the rapid buildout of AI data centers by 
reducing certain federal regulatory and permitting burdens. The administration 
did not preempt state laws on data center infrastructure development and 
federal permitting orders generally do not override state zoning and utility rules. 
In March 2026, the White House announced the Ratepayer Protection Pledge, 
under which large data center operators would voluntarily commit to covering 
the cost to upgrade generation, transmission, substations, or other necessary 
infrastructure to service these developments. The industry’s response to these 
proposals has been largely constructive. The Data Center Coalition (DCC), an 
industry group representing major hyperscalers and data center owners and 
operators came out in support of the initial version of the Pledge, while arguing 
that large-load customers across all industries also should cover the costs to 
upgrade energy infrastructure. According to the DCC, “the data center 
industry remains committed to being responsible partners where we operate, 
which includes paying our full energy costs and investing in local jobs and 
workforce development.” 
Congress voted in September 2026 to codify the principles present in the Pledge 
via the Ratepayer Protection Act. The bill, which only required states to “consider” 
these standards, passed overwhelmingly in the House (417-3), but failed to reach 
60 votes in the Senate (57-43). Critics noted the bill stopped short of imposing 
mandatory nationwide rules. 
Looking forward, we expect a Congress that is more focused on consumer 
protection might look to codify a more stringent version of these rules, requiring 
large-load customers to bear the full incremental costs associated with 
generation, transmission, and distribution upgrades. However, even if such a 
framework were passed into law, we believe the overall economic impact to large 
data center operators would be relatively modest, for two reasons. 
For one, the White House would maintain a veto over bills that include language 
it views as overly burdensome towards AI infrastructure development. Two, data 
center operators generally underwrite projects only when they have a high 
degree of confidence in securing power and, where necessary, may already be 
embedding the cost of upgrading energy infrastructure into their investment 
plans. We expect developers to be capable of passing on sustained increases in 
power infrastructure costs to their customers, which, for many 100 MW+ 
developments, are the major investment-grade hyperscalers.

### Page 6

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 6

Though data center development is receiving increasing national attention, it is 
still largely a local issue, and the most consequential elections will be at the state 
and local levels. We have seen a number of state-driven actions on data centers 
in recent months, including: 
• In July, New York State’s governor issued an executive order to establish 
a temporary moratorium on data center development while the state 
develops a standardized framework of rules for key issues (including 
responsibilities for power and energy infrastructure upgrades; additional 
power generation; clean energy; water use, emission, and waste 
management; and state/local financial incentives). The New York 
legislature also passed a one-year moratorium on Department of 
Environmental Conservation permits and approvals for 20 MW+ data 
center developments. 
• In August, Texas’ governor directed the state’s public utility commission 
to conduct a comprehensive audit of every data center advancing 
through the ERCOT (Texas’s grid operator) interconnection process. The 
audit will cover several notable areas including public financial support, 
electricity demand and supply, water use, community impacts, and 
ownership and control. 
• In September, California’s governor passed a comprehensive regulatory 
package, covering electricity, water, reporting, and environmental 
review. Most of these provisions take effect January 1, 2027, and phase 
in through 2027-2028, but the package does not ban data centers or 
impose a statewide moratorium on development activity. 
• Oregon, Pennsylvania, New Jersey, and other states have also 
established orders to create standards and frameworks governing data 
center development (but without imposing broad moratoriums). 
At the more local level, a large number of moratoriums have been passed on new 
data center development. Data collected by SemiAnalysis, an independent 
research firm, shows that, as of September, more than 300 local data center 
moratoriums have been adopted nationwide across counties, municipalities, 
townships, and other local governments.  
However, these state and local moratoriums appear thus far to be less impactful 
to near- and medium-term development activity than headline coverage might 
otherwise suggest. According to SemiAnalysis, only about 2.3 GW of the 38 GW 
of data center capacity planned or under construction for 2027 has faced delays 
as a result of the New York state executive order (0.8 GW) and/or local 
moratoriums (1.5 GW). 
We believe the primary reasons that moratoriums are, in reality, less likely to 
impact near-term development activity are (1) that these moratoriums largely 
impact new projects and applications (rather than existing developments in 
progress), (2) there may be flexibility to relocate or rezone early-stage projects in 
response to moratoriums, and (3) data center developers are increasingly 
partnering with local communities to gain their support via investments and other 
programs (as we wrote about recently). Potential regulatory risk has not yet 
slowed capex spending patterns, with capex spend still expected to climb from

### Page 7

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 7

$0.5 trillion in 2025 to $1.3 trillion by 2027; data centers typically represent ~20-
30% of the total bill of materials for new AI capacity.  
Although the practical impact on near- and medium-term development pipelines 
appears less pronounced than headlines might suggest, all this is not to say that 
data centers will not face increasing local opposition following the midterms. 
Indeed, in our conversations with industry participants, there is an 
acknowledgment that greater public scrutiny of data centers is not going away. 
Additional moratoriums, zoning restrictions, permitting requirements, or utility-
related conditions could affect the pace, cost, and overall feasibility of future 
projects.

### Page 8

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 8

Affordability Agenda: Healthcare, Housing, and 
Education Loans  
The affordability agenda has become a centerpiece of many Democratic as well 
as Republican campaign platforms. This platform is impacting investor 
debates across healthcare, housing, and student loans. In healthcare, the push 
among some Democrats for expanded health coverage under the Medicare for All 
banner is placing pressure on managed care stocks, but meanwhile, companies 
with exposure to Medicaid and the Affordable Care Act (ACA) could see a 
supportive political backdrop. On housing, investor debates on the impacts of the 
affordability agenda have continued since passage of legislation in July 2026 that 
caps the number of single-family homes for-profit institutional investors can 
purchase. Affordability of higher education is likewise part of the conversation, 
leading investors to debate which levers Congress could pull that would affect 
student loan companies.  
Healthcare 
In healthcare, we see the upcoming midterm elections as a sentiment event for 
managed care stocks, not a policy event. With the presidential veto in place 
through 2028, we do not expect the government to enact direct healthcare 
legislation during the next Congress. Forecasts place the probability of a change 
in House control at roughly 79%, with both chambers changing in about 44% of 
simulations. Although investor skittishness around Medicare for All (M4A) rhetoric 
is high, we see the likelihood of M4A replacing private coverage as a near-zero 
probability in this timeframe.  
M4A has no realistic legislative path before 2029, but the rhetoric weighs on 
managed care organization (MCO) stock multiples by raising perceived long-term 
risk rather than affecting near-term earnings. Polling from Kaiser Family 
Foundation, a health policy research organization, shows support for M4A at a 
decade-plus high, and the 2018–2019 cycle suggests that increased campaign 
attention can pressure MCO multiples even without a direct policy impact. We 
expect similar sentiment-driven pressure through year-end and potentially into 
the 2028 primary cycle but would not view it as a change in near-term 
fundamentals.  
For Medicare Advantage, we see a headline risk over earnings reality. The near-
term earnings impact for affected companies should be limited, because the US 
Centers for Medicare & Medicaid Services (CMS) has finalized a 2.48% Medicare 
Advantage (MA) rate increase for 2027, versus 0.09% in the advance notice. A 
change in House control would instead increase focus on 2028 payment policy, 
risk adjustment, and prior authorization. Coding reform already has bipartisan 
support: sponsors estimate that the Cassidy-Merkley No UPCODE Act could 
generate up to $275 billion of savings over ten years, while MedPAC estimates 
coding intensity will add roughly $40 billion to 2025 Medicare Advantage costs 
relative to traditional Medicare. In our view, the more immediate consideration is 
potential multiple pressure rather than a measurable change to company 
earnings.  
However, the next Congress could benefit Medicaid funding and ACA policy. 
Medicaid and the ACA appear relatively better positioned, although any benefit

### Page 9

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 9

would likely be reflected first in sentiment and lower perceived policy risk rather 
than near-term estimate revisions. Medicaid community engagement 
requirements take effect in most states by January 1, 2027. Although repeal 
remains unlikely given the presidential veto, a change in House control could shift 
the year-end funding debate toward restoring or preserving federal Medicaid 
support, which could ease state budget pressure and support rate-setting. The 
ACA policy backdrop could also become more supportive, although we see 
limited scope for enhanced premium subsidies to return before 2029 after the 
three-year extension passed by the House stalled.  
Housing 
Turning to the housing sector, the likely midterm outcome of a split government 
would result in more gridlock. Historically, political gridlock in terms of passing 
new regulation has been a tailwind for the real estate sector, reducing regulatory 
risk at the federal level. In the scenario of a split Congress, in which the Democrats 
have a majority in the House but not the Senate, bills with bipartisan support have 
a greater likelihood to become law. Two key areas in which this bipartisan support 
exists are housing affordability and consumer protection. 
Among real estate investment trusts (REITs), we see Residential as among the 
most impacted subsectors, particularly as it relates to the affordability debate. 
Regardless of the election outcome, housing affordability has become front and 
center on the political agenda across both parties. Federal legislation is more 
likely to target supply-side solutions, as legislation restricting rent increases is 
typically handled at the local and state level. We think Multifamily would be the 
most likely subsector impacted, as a push to expand rental housing supply could 
lead to eased zoning restrictions, particularly in supply-constrained coastal 
markets that have historically benefited from higher barriers to entry. That said, 
this would likely take years to materialize. With a Democratic majority, Congress 
could voice opposition to the influence of large institutional landlords, but we see 
this strictly as a sentiment risk to stocks.  
Single Family For Rent (SFR) is less exposed to near-term legislative shocks, as 
the sector was a focus of the 21st Century ROAD to Housing Act passed in July 
2026 over the summer. The measure impacts institutional owners’ ability to buy 
homes off the MLS but contains specific carve-out provisions that will allow them 
to keep developing build-to-rent (BTR) product and buy excess inventory from 
homebuilders, the sector's preferred acquisition channel in recent years. The 
ROAD to Housing Act also contained provisions that could theoretically ease the 
production of manufactured homes, the main business segment for 
Manufactured Housing (MH) REITs. The first-order impact is likely more flexibility 
in home designs that could lead to further densification of MH communities, 
although we note emerging investor debate about the long-term impacts of 
increased MH production on competitive supply for a property type that has 
historically been supply constrained, mainly due to NIMBY-ism.  
Parallel to the conversation on affordability, it is clear that, regardless of which 
political party may be in power following the midterms, a major focus remains 
addressing the US fiscal deficit and its continued growth. We continue to pay 
attention to potential budget cuts or sequestration to slow government spending 
and reduce the deficit, which could have an impact on real estate. Key areas to 
watch include cuts to healthcare spending (which would be a negative for skilled

### Page 10

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 10

nursing focused REITs, given Medicare is a major revenue source for their tenant 
base) and cuts that would impact Office REITs with large government exposure.  
We also worry that an elevated federal deficit and the elevated Treasury 
borrowing needs today create a risk that interest rates will remain higher for 
longer. This puts pressure on real estate valuations and cost of financing for REITs 
to drive external growth via acquisitions and development. A split Congress is 
likely to result in more difficult budget negotiations and increased oversight, but 
it remains unclear what the ultimate outcome would be to address the budget 
deficit issue. 
With real estate being more dependent on local market dynamics, we believe the 
more likely regulatory impact would come from local regulation. This could show 
up on the ballots during the midterms, with increased possibility of rent control 
legislation targeted at residential real estate as well as self-storage, as cities and 
municipalities focus on affordability issues. There is also growing legislative 
pushback at the local level against development of real assets such as data 
centers given the large power requirements, as discussed in detail elsewhere in 
this report.  
Education Lending 
Midterm campaign platforms on education affordability have a bearing on the 
consumer and specialty finance sector. Much of the policy exposure in this sector 
is through the executive branch bodies, not Congress, however. This includes, for 
example, regulatory authorities such as the FDIC (Federal Deposit Insurance 
Corporation), the CFPB (Consumer Financial Protection Bureau), or the FHFA 
(Federal Housing Finance Agency, which regulates Fannie Mae and Freddie 
Mac). That said, historical data shows that student loan company stocks can still 
directionally align with midterm party outcome and expectations in Congress, 
although the sample size is small.

### Page 11

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 11

Mixed Impacts of Economic and Fiscal Policy  
Democratic and Republican candidates have aimed to distinguish themselves 
from their opponents along a range of economic and fiscal policy matters, 
including tariffs, tax, labor, and the budget. In reality, the outcome of the 
midterms, regardless of which party prevails, is likely to present a mixture of relief 
and restrictions affecting businesses. In some cases, investor fears over sweeping 
economic policy changes are overblown for topics over which Congress 
realistically has limited influence, presenting more headline risk than a risk to 
fundamentals. In considering these, we look to industrials that are particularly 
sensitive to key investor debates, spanning multi-industry/electrical equipment, 
clean tech and alternative energy, transport, and defense.  
For the multi-industry and electrical equipment (MI/EE) sector, historical midterm 
election cycles suggest a generally constructive backdrop heading into election 
day, with positive returns in six of the past seven elections. On average, the MI/EE 
group has gained ~5% during the three months preceding historical midterm 
elections and ~8% during the final month prior. Relative outperformance versus 
the S&P 500 has been less consistent; we observed outperformance in only four 
of seven cycles, though the group still generated average excess returns of 
~250bps over the preceding three months and ~270bps over the preceding 
month.  
Overall, the data supports a favorable absolute return backdrop ahead of 
midterms, but significant stock-level dispersion suggests company-specific 
fundamentals and factor leadership have historically mattered more than the 
election cycle alone. We mapped out election results scenarios across key policy 
pillars for our coverage (tax policy and capital investment; trade, tariffs, and 
supply chains; energy, grid, and power infrastructure; AI and data center 
regulation; and the regulatory environment). We focus on two key scenarios: one 
in which Republicans maintain control of both chambers, and a divided 
government that sees the Democrats controlling one or both chambers of the 
legislative branch.  
Under a divided government, we expect stock-level dispersion would likely 
narrow, with globally exposed companies receiving some trade policy relief and 
HVAC/automation companies benefiting from a potentially more supportive 
regulatory backdrop. However, should Republicans maintain control of Congress, 
our scenario analysis suggests this would be favorable for electrical equipment 
and power infrastructure manufacturers, due to a combination of pro-investment 
tax policy, faster infrastructure deployment, and a lighter AI regulatory 
framework. That said, globally exposed companies could face incremental tariff 
and retaliation risks.  
Looking to the clean tech and alternative energy sector, the midterm elections 
could represent a positive tailwind. The next Congress might be positioned to 
pursue targeted extensions or modifications to certain clean-energy tax credits. 
The introduction of H.R. 8477, which proposes to restore several clean energy tax 
credits rolled back in late 2025, seems to signal that some, albeit a limited 
number, of Republicans could be supportive of this initiative. If the Democrats 
gain a majority in the House but not the Senate in the midterms, it is plausible that 
Democrats could find sufficient Republican support to advance

### Page 12

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 12

legislation extending clean-energy credits, though they would still face 
significant legislative hurdles. A midterm outcome in which Republicans retain 
control of both chambers is likely to maintain the status quo for the remaining 
existing tax credits, especially for those tied to domestic manufacturing. While 
those could potentially see further extension, we think this is a limited outcome.    
While we see a low probability of a major change in, or support for, the residential 
tax credits that expired at the end of 2025 (25D), we envision a scenario in which 
indirect residential tax credits (48E) could be extended, which would help boost 
part of the demand. Softness in demand is expected to remain going into 2027, 
still impacted by the phaseout of the 25D tax credits, the eventual roll-off of safe-
harbor benefits, and a more constrained financing environment.  
From a manufacturing tax credit standpoint, there could be additional tailwinds 
if we were to see any manufacturing tax credit extension or positive modification 
(although lower probability, in our view). This would also likely improve utility-
scale project economics if broader project-related credits are preserved, though 
upcoming elections could cause a slowdown in new booking activity until 
legislative uncertainty clears.  
For Transports, the midterms would be most relevant for the trucking supply 
thesis. Truckload stocks could have a modest negative reaction to a Democratic 
majority if investors expect some rollback of the Trump administration’s 
constraints on drivers, which has tightened supply. We would separate 
perception from fundamentals, however. The election of a Democratic Congress 
would not automatically by itself change leadership at the Department of 
Transportation (DOT) and the Federal Motor Carrier Safety Administration 
(FMCSA), the executive branch entities driving much of the current push for 
restrictions on drivers.  
One possible impact of the midterms for Transports is if a change in 
Congressional control contributed to a faster resolution of the war in Iran. Any 
resulting lower energy prices would be a positive for Transports. We view this as 
a lower-probability outcome, however, given the constraints on a divided 
government.  
With clashes over the budget likely looming, aerospace and defense could get 
caught in the crosshairs. While defense spending has historically enjoyed 
bipartisan support, it has grown politicized following the war in Iran, making it 
more difficult for a split Congress to pass such a budget. The defense budget has 
experienced significant growth over the last few years, rising from $900 billion in 
FY2024 to $1 trillion in FY2026. The Trump administration would like to see this 
trend accelerate: in April 2026, the president issued a budget request that called 
for a $1.5 trillion budget for the Department of Defense in FY2027. With a 
continuing resolution likely into year-end, the outcome of the midterm elections 
will determine whether the next Congress can pass such a budget. If Republicans 
hold the House and Senate, we think there is a reasonable prospect of a budget 
of $1.2 trillion or more passing into law. Defense investors would see that as a 
substantial tailwind for beaten-up defense stocks. On the other hand, this would 
be unlikely in a split Congress, in our view. Given all this, we see the outcome of 
the upcoming midterm elections as key to determining how US defense stocks 
will perform into year end and beyond.

### Page 13

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 13

Taking Stock of the Known Unknowns 
Although Republicans could maintain control of both chambers, consensus is 
converging around polls pointing to a Democratic majority in the House and 
possibly the Senate. The perceived certainty has allowed the investor debate 
related to midterms to focus not only on who will win but how they will govern. In 
some cases, headlines are misrepresenting what a Congress constrained by the 
executive will be able to accomplish. In other areas, legislation that is expected 
to be passed will need to be taken together with offsetting factors. Whatever the 
outcome, the political backdrop will continue to influence the market and 
investor debates in the year ahead.

### Page 14

Deutsche Bank Research Institute 
Swing State: Navigating Midterm Volatility – October 23, 2026

トランプ政権に逆風、AI・半導体株の勝ち組は変わ
るのか 
～米中間選挙で揺らぐ AI投資と個人投資家の選択～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  柏村 祐

（要旨） 
○ 2026年 11月の米中間選挙における民主党の躍進（共和党の敗北）を受け、AI・半導体株が
急落している。しかし、半導体株の調整は選挙前からすでに始まっており、巨額の AI投資に
対する収益化への疑念に、政治的・規制的な不確実性が加わった結果とみるべきである。 
○ 中間選挙を経て、AI開発の物理的基盤であるデータセンターに対する規制強化が新たなリス
クとして浮上している。米国では地域社会の反対などにより、データセンターの開発停止措
置（モラトリアム）が急増しており、インフラ制約が成長シナリオのボトルネックとなりつ
つある。 
○ 政治的逆風やインフラ制約が意識されるなか、今後の AI・半導体株の選別において重要性が
一段と高まる指標は「利益成長力」である。2027年の予想 EPS修正率を見ると、半導体・ハ
ードウェアといった上流工程の利益予想は大きく上方修正されている一方、巨大クラウド企
業や電力関連では上方修正幅が小さい。 
○ 個人投資家にとっては、「AI銘柄の全面高」から利益成長力に基づく選別が重要な局面へと
移りつつあることを認識し、ポートフォリオを再点検することが求められる。期待先行の銘
柄から利益成長の裏付けを持つ銘柄へのシフトと、規制リスクの直撃を受けるセクターの再
点検が、実務上の対応策となるだろう。

1. 共和党敗北の衝撃とAI株の動揺 
2026年11月の米中間選挙は、金融市場に大きな衝撃を与えた。民主党が予想以上の規模で躍進
し、トランプ大統領率いる共和党が上下両院で敗北を喫したことで、金融市場では政治・規制環
境の変化に対する警戒感が高まった。とりわけ投資家の関心を集めているのが、これまで相場を
牽引してきたAI（人工知能）・半導体株の急落である。 
中間選挙直後の11月4日、米国株式市場はいったん上昇したものの、翌 5日には一転して急落
した。なかでもAIインフラを支える半導体やデータセンター関連銘柄の下げ幅は際立っていた。
次期議会で主導権を握る民主党が、データセンターの建設規制や巨大テック企業への監視を強化

### Page 2

2/6 
するとの懸念が広がったことも、下落の背景にあるとみられる。 
マクロ環境の不確実性が高まり、ボラティリティ（価格変動）が高まるなか、個人投資家は「A
Iテーマ」とどう向き合い、ポートフォリオをどう再構築すべきなのか。 
本稿では、選挙前後の株価動向、データセンター規制、業種別の利益予想の 3点をデータで確
認し、個人投資家の資産運用上の論点を整理する。

2. 共和党敗北とAI・半導体株の現在地 
(1) 中間選挙後、AI・半導体株の下落は加速したのか 
2026年11月の米中間選挙における民主党の躍進（共和党の敗北）は、株式市場、とりわけAI・
半導体株に大きな波紋を広げた。選挙直後の 11月 5日（木曜日）、米国の主要株価指数は急落
し、ナスダック総合指数は－5.5％、S&P500種株価指数は－4.4％の下落を記録した。特に半導体
やデータセンター関連銘柄の下落幅は、市場全体を大きく上回るものとなった。民主党が主導権
を握る新たな政治シナリオのもとで、AIインフラ構築に対する規制強化や監視の目が厳しくなる
との懸念が、投資家心理を冷やしたとみられる。 
しかし、この「AI株安」を単なる選挙結果のショックとして片付けるべきではない。State 
Street Investment Managementの分析によれば、AI関連株の調整は中間選挙の前からすでに始ま
っていた。事実、世界の半導体株は選挙前の段階で、直近の高値から約20％の下落（ドローダウ
ン）を経験していた（資料 1）。巨額のAI設備投資に対するリターン（収益化）への疑念や、高止
まりする業績期待に対するハードルがすでに高まっていたところに、中間選挙による政治的・規
制的な不確実性が加わり、既存の価格調整（リプライシング）が加速したとみるのが自然であろ
う。

### Page 3

3/6 
資料 1 過去 5年間の半導体株の下落率（ドローダウン） 
（注）緑線は世界の半導体株（MSCI AC World Semiconductor & Semiconductor Equipment Industry）、青面は米国株全体
（S&P500）の直近高値からの下落率（％）を示す。 
（出所）State Street Investment Management, “Midterms add to an AI correction already underway”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所作成

(2) データセンター規制はAI・半導体株の新たなリスクになるのか 
中間選挙を経て新たなリスクとして注目されているのが、AI開発の物理的基盤であるデータセ
ンターに対する規制強化である。米国では選挙前から電力需要の逼迫や環境負荷を懸念する地域
社会の反対が強まっており、民主党の躍進によって、こうした懸念が政策に反映される可能性が
高まっている。 
IEA（国際エネルギー機関）の報告書によれば、米国におけるデータセンターの開発停止措置
（モラトリアム）は急増している。2026年 1～7月に導入された開発停止措置の件数は、2025年
通年の約5倍に達した（資料 2）。地域住民からの反対理由は、土地利用や水資源・環境への影
響、電力価格の高騰懸念など多岐にわたる。こうした地域社会の反対を背景に、2024年 1月から
2026年5月半ばまでの間に、米国で開発が阻止、撤回、または停滞したAIデータセンター事業は
46件に上り、これらの事業に関連する発表ベースの投資額は合計 1,700億ドルを超えている。AI
ブームの足元で、物理的なインフラ制約と規制リスクが、関連企業の成長シナリオに対する明確
なボトルネックとして浮上している。

### Page 4

4/6 
資料 2 米国における懸念事項別のデータセンター開発停止措置 
（注）米国における州、郡、市町村レベルでのデータセンター開発停止措置（モラトリアム）の件数。複数の懸念事項が挙げられて
いる場合は均等に分割して集計。2026年は 1～7月のデータ。 
（出所）IEA, “Powering Artificial Intelligence”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(3) AI・半導体株の勝ち組は利益成長力で決まるのか 
政治的逆風やインフラ制約が意識されるなか、今後のAI・半導体株の選別において、重要性が
一段と高まるのが「利益成長力」である。マクロ環境の不確実性が高まる局面では、期待先行で
買われた銘柄は淘汰され、確かな業績の裏付けを持つ企業に資金が集中しやすい。 
J.P. Morgan Asset Managementの分析によれば、AIバリューチェーンのなかでも業種によって
将来の利益成長期待に明確な濃淡が表れている。2026年10月 29日時点における2027年の予想EP
S（1株当たり利益）について、2025年末からの修正率を見ると、半導体・半導体製造装置（Semi
s）が＋14.6％と突出して高い上方修正を示している（資料 3）。次いで、通信機器などのハードウ
ェアが＋4.5％となっている。一方で、AI投資を牽引する巨大クラウド企業（Hyperscalers）は＋
1.5％、ソフトウェアは＋0.6％にとどまり、電力関連（Power）に至っては 0.0％と上方修正が見
られない。 
これは、AIの最終的な需要（エンドユーザーへの普及や収益化）には依然として不透明感が残
るものの、その手前にある「計算資源の確保」という物理的な制約を解消するための上流工程
（半導体やハードウェア）については、強い需要が見込まれていることを示している。中間選挙
後の新たな市場環境において、個人投資家にはAIテーマをひと括りにするのではなく、規制リス
クに加え、利益成長の持続性や株価の割高感を見極めながら、「真の勝ち組」を選別する視点が求
められている。

### Page 5

5/6

資料 3 2026年および 2027年の予想 EPS修正率（業種別） 
（注）2025年 12月末時点からのコンセンサス予想 EPS（1株当たり利益）の修正率。巨大クラウド企業（Hyperscalers）は
Amazon、Alphabet、Meta、Microsoft、Oracleの加重平均。電力関連は電気機器および電力公益事業の加重平均。 
（出所）J.P. Morgan Asset Management, “3Q26 Earnings Bulletin”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

3. 「AI株選別時代」の資産防衛 
第 2章で見てきたように、AI・半導体株を取り巻く環境は「期待先行の全面高」から、規制リ
スクと利益成長力が綱引きをする「選別のフェーズ」へと移りつつある。選挙前から広がってい
たデータセンターの開発停止措置（モラトリアム）に加え、民主党の躍進による規制強化への警
戒感が高まる一方で、半導体やハードウェアといった上流工程の利益成長期待は引き続き根強
い。 
ただし、これはAIブームの終わりを意味しない。個人投資家に求められるのは、第 1章で提起
した通り、新たな環境に合わせてポートフォリオを再構築することである。実務上の対応策とし
て、以下の2点が挙げられる。 
第一に、これまでの期待先行から実需（利益成長）へのシフトである。これまではAIというテ
ーマ性だけで買われていた銘柄も多かったが、今後は業績による選別が進むと考えられる。デー
タセンター規制などの影響を見極めつつ、需要と利益成長の裏付けを持つ半導体や通信インフラ
関連のハードウェア企業へ、投資の比重を移すことが有効な選択肢となる。J.P. Morgan Asset 
Managementのデータが示す通り、2027年の利益予想が大きく上方修正されている領域が候補とな
る。ただし、上方修正がすでに株価に織り込まれていないかは銘柄ごとに確認する必要がある。 
第二に、規制リスクの直撃を受けるセクターの再点検である。AI投資の恩恵を受けると期待さ
れてきた電力や一部の不動産（データセンターREITなど）は、地域社会の反対や環境規制の強化
によって、想定通りの成長シナリオを描けなくなるリスク（投資回収の遅れや資産価値の低下に
つながるリスク）が浮上している。これらの銘柄を保有している場合は、政策動向を注視し、必

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
要に応じてウェイトを引き下げるなどのリスク管理が必要になる。 
同じ AI関連でも、2027年の予想EPS修正率は半導体・半導体製造装置の＋14.6％から電力関連
の0.0％まで開いている。加えて、地域社会の反対を背景に阻止、撤回、または停滞したAIデー
タセンター事業は46件、発表ベースの投資額で 1,700億ドル超に上る。中間選挙後は、利益予想
が切り上がっているか、規制の影響を受けやすい事業かという 2点で保有銘柄を点検すること
が、個人投資家の資産防衛の出発点になるだろう。

## Document 530: 世界的なインフレ環境と｢異空間｣にある中国の物価動向 ～企業部門ではインフレも、家計部門はディスインフレが続き、金融政策_4eb5b875

Source PDF: all report pdf/世界的なインフレ環境と｢異空間｣にある中国の物価動向 ～企業部門ではインフレも、家計部門はディスインフレが続き、金融政策_4eb5b875.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 11 月 9 日

1/3 
世界的なインフレ環境と｢異空間｣にある中国の物価動向 
～企業部門ではインフレも、家計部門はディスインフレが続き、金融政策の自由度も低下～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ イラン情勢の悪化に伴うエネルギー価格の上昇を受け、世界的にインフレが加速し、金融引
き締めの動きが広がっている。中国でも原材料価格の上昇を背景に、10 月の生産者物価は購
入価格、出荷価格ともに2022 年６月以来の高い伸びとなり、企業部門ではコスト上昇圧力が
強まっている。一方、消費者物価は生鮮品を中心とする食料品価格の下落や内需の弱さを背
景に伸びが鈍化しており、企業部門がインフレ、家計部門がディスインフレに直面する二極
化が続いている。こうした状況は企業収益を圧迫するとともに、不動産市場を巡る懸念も重
なり、株式相場の重しとなっている。中国当局は株式市場の下支え策を打ち出しているもの
の、市場マインドの改善には力不足とみられる。世界的な金融引き締めが進むなか、資金流
出や人民元安への懸念から大幅な金融緩和にも踏み切りにくく、中国当局は景気下支えと金
融市場の安定の両立に向けて難しい政策運営を迫られている。

イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油をはじめとする商品市況の上昇は、世界的なエネルギー価格の上
昇を招いている。さらに、エネルギー価格の上昇は幅広い経済活動においてコスト上昇を引き起こ
しており、多くの国でインフレが加速する動きがみられる。その結果、米国やユーロ圏、日本をは
じめとする先進国のみならず、新興国においても中央銀行が金融引き締めに動く流れが広がりをみ
せている。このように、世界的に金融政策を取り巻く環境は大きく変化している。 
中国においても、企業部門はエネルギーインフレの影響を免れない状況にある。川上段階の物価
にあたる 10 月の生産者物価（購入価格）は、前年同月比＋7.9％と前月（同＋6.9％）から加速し
て、2022 年６月以来の高い伸びとなった（図１）。前月比は＋1.1％と前月（同＋1.2％）からわず
かに上昇ペースは鈍化したものの、３ヵ月連続で上昇するなど、物価上昇圧力に直面している。原
油・天然ガスなどエネルギー関連で物価上昇圧力が強まるとともに、原材料価格の上昇を反映して

2/3 
化学工業関連などで上昇基調が続いて
いることが影響している。こうした原材
料価格の上昇を反映して、生産者物価
（出荷価格）も前年同月比＋5.7％と前
月（同＋5.0％）から加速して、2022 年

＋0.7％と前月（同＋1.2％）からペース
は鈍化したものの、３ヵ月連続で上昇し
ており、中間財を中心に価格上昇の動き
が確認されるなど、企業間取引段階では
価格転嫁の動きが広がっている。ただ
し、生活資料価格は前月比で下落しており、耐久消費財価格も下落するなど、最終消費財への価格
転嫁のハードルの高さがうかがえる。 
一方で、足元ではエルニーニョ現象の発生を受けて、世界的に異常気象の動きがみられるなか、
イラン情勢の悪化による肥料価格の高止まりも重なり、農業生産の低迷が食料インフレを引き起こ

続の横ばいで推移しており、前述の通り企業部門が製品価格にコスト上昇を転嫁できないことに加
え、雇用環境が厳しさを増すなかでサービス物価も上昇圧力に乏しい。

3/3 
足元の中国では、企業部門がインフレに、家計部門がディスインフレに直面する『異世界』が続
いている。企業部門にとって、コスト上昇を製品価格に転嫁することが難しい状況は、収益の圧迫
につながっている。そのうえ、不動産開発企業を巡る事業環境の悪化や債務問題の再燃に対する懸
念も重なり、関連株を中心に下押し圧力
がかかるなど、株式相場の重しとなって
いる（図３）。こうしたなか、中国人民銀
行（中銀）と国家金融監督管理総局、中
国証券監督管理委員会は２日、証券・基
金・保険会社向けのスワップ制度の拡充
や、株式買い戻し・買い増し向け再貸出
金利の引き下げなど、株式相場の下支え
に向けた措置を打ち出した。さらに、中
国証券監督管理委員会が投資銀行に対
して新規株式公開（ＩＰＯ）の一時停止
を求めるとともに、既存株主による大口の株式売却を制限したとも報じられた。しかし、こうした
対応は既存の政策枠組みの拡充が中心であり、冷え込んだ市場マインドを好転させるには力不足と
受け止められている模様である。世界的に金融引き締めの動きが広がりをみせるなか、中国当局に
とっては資金流出や人民元安への懸念から大幅な金融緩和に動くことも難しく、景気下支えと金融
市場の安定の両立に向けて難しい政策の舵取りを迫られる局面が続くことになろう。

## Document 537: 中国：ユニコーンの勃興とK字型経済の行方

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国：ユニコーンの勃興とK字型経済の行方.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

2026 年 11 月 20 日 全 5 頁

中国：ユニコーンの勃興と K 字型経済の行方 
雇用に悪影響の場合、一部産業の速すぎる発展を抑制する可能性も

調査本部 主席研究員 齋藤 尚登

[要約] 
 「上海胡潤百富投資管理諮詢」の「2026 年グローバルユニコーンランキング」による
と、世界のグローバルユニコーン企業は合計 1,603 社で、うち米国がおよそ半分の 806
社、中国が約 1/4 の 381 社を数えた。中国のグローバルユニコーンの業種別内訳を見
ると、社数では、人工知能（AI）、半導体、新エネルギー、生命科学、ロボットなどが
上位に並ぶ。これは国家が重点に指定した産業と重複するものが多く、国家の産業政
策が重要な役割を果たしていることが示唆される。 
 中国でグローバルユニコーンが集積しているのは、北京市、上海市、広東省深圳市、
浙江省杭州市、広東省広州市などである。この 5 都市には、①中国屈指の大学を有し、
科学技術の発展・イノベーションを担う優秀な人材を輩出している、②国家レベルの
「千人計画」、深圳市の「孔雀計画」、杭州市の「人材新政」など、海外で博士号などを
取得した中国人研究者の呼び戻し政策が奏功している、③リーディングカンパニーが
存在し、その企業からのスピンオフ、もしくは当該企業からの出資などによって、特
徴のある産業の蓄積がなされる、④北京証券取引所、上海証券取引所、深圳証券取引
所といった「出口」が活用できる、などの共通点がある。 
 中国では、新興産業が勃興する一方で、内需依存型の伝統産業の凋落を補い切れてい
ない。「K字型」の経済構造が鮮明化しているが、上向きの矢印が細く、下向きのそれ
が太いため、成長率の低下が続いているのが現状だ。そして、この状況はしばらく続
く可能性が高い。というのは、例えば、AI やロボットなど中国が強みを持つ分野は、
雇用を代替する可能性が高いからだ。当局には絶妙なバランスが求められ、雇用が悪
化する場合は、一部産業の速すぎる発展を抑制する政策を打ち出す可能性も否定でき
ない。

グローバルユニコーンランキング、中国は世界 2 位を維持 
中国の調査会社「上海胡潤百富投資管理諮詢（以下、胡潤百富）」の定義によると、グロー
バルユニコーンとは、2000 年以降創業の比較的若い企業で、企業価値評価額 10 億ドル（算出
基準は 2026 年 1 月 1 日で、1 ドル＝6.8 元で換算）以上の非上場会社である。同社の「2026 年
グローバルユニコーンランキング」によると、世界のグローバルユニコーン企業は合計 1,603
社で、うち米国がおよそ半分の 806 社、中国が約 1/4 の 381 社を数える（図表 1）。2026 年の 3
位は英国の 80 社だ。日本は 9 社にとどまり、順位は 14 位であった。残念ながら日本の社数は
2024 年から増えていない。 
中国のグローバルユニコーンの数は米国に差を開けられているものの、順位は長期的に 2 位
を維持している。中国のグローバルユニコーン企業トップ 10 は図表 2 の通りであり、動画共有
アプリ TikTok を運営するバイトダンス（企業価値評価額は 3 兆 2,600 億元、約 77.6 兆円。1 元
＝23.8 円で換算）が中国最大のユニコーンであり、世界でも 3 位にランキングされている（世
界 1 位は米アンソロピックの 6 兆 5,600 億元 1）。以下、アリババ集団の金融会社であるアント
グループ、低価格ファッションの EC 通販のシーイン、そして生成 AI のディープシークなど、
日本でもよく知られた企業が上位に並ぶ。ちなみに、シーインは 2026 年 9 月 1 日に香港のメイ
ンボードに上場しており、2027 年以降はランキングの対象から外れる。 
以下では、中国が世界有数のグローバルユニコーン輩出国となった背景を明らかにすべく、
中国のグローバルユニコーンの特徴について、まとめたい。

国家の産業政策の重要性 
図表 3 は 2026 年の中国のグローバルユニコーンの業種別内訳（社数と企業価値評価額）を見
たものであり、社数では、人工知能（AI）、半導体、新エネルギー、生命科学、ロボットなど
が、企業価値評価額では、メディア・エンターテイメント、AI、フィンテック、半導体、電子
商取引（e コマース）などが上位に並ぶ。 
これは国家が重点に指定した産業と重複するものが多い。例えば、「戦略的新興産業」では、
かねて新世代情報技術、新エネルギー、新素材、スマート新エネルギー自動車、ロボット、バ
イオ、ハイエンド設備製造、航空宇宙などが重視され、2026 年から始まった第 15 次 5 カ年計
画では、新興基幹産業として、集積回路、航空宇宙、バイオ医薬、低空経済 2、新型エネルギ
ー貯蔵、スマートロボットへの注力が謳われた。「未来産業」は、製造、情報、材料、エネル
ギー、宇宙空間、ヘルスケアのそれぞれの先端分野であり、第 15 次 5 カ年計画期間中は、量子
技術、バイオ製造、水素エネルギーと核融合エネルギー、BMI（ブレインマシーンインターフ
ェース）、エンボディド AI（具身知能）、第 6 世代移動通信（6G）などが重点とされた。こうし
た産業には、中央・地方政府、国有・民営企業を問わず、傾斜投資が行われる可能性が高い。
国家の産業政策が重要な役割を果たしているのだ。

グローバルユニコーン集積地である 5 大都市の共通点 
 中国でグローバルユニコーンが集積しているのは、北京市（2026 年は 86 社）、上海市（74
社）、広東省深圳市（44 社）、浙江省杭州市（25 社）、広東省広州市（24 社）である。この 5 都
市合計は 253 社となり、全体（381 社）の 66.4％を占める。 
 この 5 都市にはいくつかの共通点がある。具体的には、①経済が発展し、技術の蓄積が進ん

2 低空経済とは、地表から高度 1,000m までの低空域でドローンや電動垂直離着機（eVTOL）などを活用して生
まれる経済活動のこと。

でいること、②北京市は清華大学、北京大学、上海市は復旦大学、上海交通大学、深圳市は南
方科技大学、杭州市は浙江大学、広州市は中山大学といった中国屈指の大学を有し、科学技術
の発展・イノベーションを担う優秀な人材を輩出していること、③国家レベルの「千人計画」、
深圳市の「孔雀計画」、杭州市の「人材新政」など、海外で博士号などを取得した中国人研究
者や、先端技術分野の企業人材などを中国（当該都市）に呼び戻し、国家レベルの研究プロジ
ェクトや大学・研究機関の中核人材に据える計画が奏功したこと 3、④いずれの都市にも国家
レベルの「高新技術産業開発区」が設置され、税金・費用の減免など手厚い政策サポートを受
けていること、⑤それぞれの都市にリーディングカンパニー4が存在し、その企業からのスピン
オフ、もしくは当該企業からの出資などによって、特徴のある産業の蓄積がなされること、⑥
北京証券取引所（2021 年 11 月に開設された技術力の高い中小企業向けの株式市場）、上海証券
取引所（メインボード、科創板＝2019 年 7 月に開設されたハイテク企業向けの新しい株式市
場）、深圳証券取引所といった「出口」が活用できること 5、などを挙げることができる。

多層的な資金調達が機能 
グローバルユニコーンの「出口」のひとつが株式の上場であるが、初期段階の資金調達で重
要な役割を果たすのが、ベンチャーキャピタルである。ベンチャーキャピタル関連のデータサ
ービス企業である IT 桔子によると、中国のベンチャーキャピタルは 2026 年 3 月 10 日時点で、
6,529 社とされるが、休眠中のものも多く、2015 年から 2025 年までの間に実際に投資を行った
ベンチャーキャピタルは 737 社とされる。中でも存在感が大きいのは、IDG 資本投資顧問（北
京 ）、 紅杉資本中国基金、深圳市創新投資、金沙江創業投資、経緯創投（北京）投資管理顧問
などである 6。このほか、有力なリーディングカンパニーによる出資も多い。 
リーディングカンパニーの後ろ盾のないスタートアップ企業が銀行融資を受けるのは困難で
ある。ある程度の企業規模を有するようになった後に、そのハードルを下げる役割を果たすの
が、政府部門などによる認定である。例えば、中国工業・情報化部が管轄するイノベーション
型中小企業、専精特新中小企業などがそれに該当する。省レベルの専精特新中小企業とは、専
門性・精密（精細）性・独自性・革新性があると認定された中小企業で、その上位ランクにあ
たるのが、国家レベルの専精特新「小巨人」企業だ。こうした政府部門の認定を受けると、銀
行融資が格段に受けやすくなる。ちなみに、既述した北京証券取引所は 2026 年 11 月 15 日に開
業 5 周年を迎えた。上場会社数は当初の 81 社から 355 社に増加し、そのうち 6 割超が国家級専
精特新「小巨人」企業によって占められている。

3 高額の研究費・生活支援など、個人やチームに資金パッケージを用意し、研究・教育・起業を支援。「孔雀
計画」では、個人に数十万元、チームに数千万元の資金をサポートするケースがあるという。 
4 例えば、北京市の百度（Bai du）、上海市の美団（Mei tuan）、深圳市の華為（Hua wei）、テンセント、杭
州市のアリババなど。 
5 杭州市は上海市と、広州市は深圳市と地理的に近い。 
6 英語名は順番に、IDG Capital、Hong Shan、Shenzhen Capital Group、GSR Ventures、MPC。

グローバルユニコーンの勃興と「K 字型」経済の行方 
以上、中国のグローバルユニコーン勃興の背景についてまとめた。上場会社になると、グロ
ーバルユニコーンを「卒業」となるわけだが、卒業生には、中国の代表的なソーシャル電子商
取引の拼多多（米ナスダック）、大手スマートフォン・スマート電気自動車メーカーの小米
（香港証券取引所）、中国最大のライフサービスプラットフォームの美団（香港証券取引所）、
世界最大の動力電池メーカーの寧徳時代（深圳証券取引所）など、そうそうたるメンバーが並
ぶ 7。いずれも 2018 年に上場を果たしている。 
中国では、新興産業が勃興する一方で、内需依存型の伝統産業の凋落を補い切れていない。
「K字型」の経済構造が鮮明化しているが、上向きの矢印が細く、下向きのそれが太いため、成
長率の低下が続いているのが現状だ。そして、この状況はしばらく続く可能性が高い。という
のは、例えば、AI やロボットなど中国が強みを持つ分野は、雇用を代替する可能性が高いから
だ。フードデリバリーなど各種デリバリーは自動運転が大々的に実装されればどうなるのか。
産前産後ケアや高齢者ケア、清掃などの仕事は、フィジカル AI が多くを代替することになろ
う。中国共産党が経済政策運営上もっとも要視する雇用への悪影響の懸念だ。当局には絶妙な
バランスが求められ、場合によっては、一部産業の速すぎる発展を抑制する政策を打ち出す可
能性も否定できない。

7 英語名は順番に、PDD Holdings、Xiaomi Corporation、Meituan、CATL。

## Document 592: 凱基投顧_2881 富邦金_施志鴻_20261116

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_2881 富邦金_施志鴻_20261116.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 16, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 金融 ‧ 台灣

富邦金 
(2881.TW/2881 TT)

調整後獲利及其他權益改善提升 2026F現金股利水準 
重要訊息 
富邦金前 10 月每股盈餘年增 17%至 8.75 元，但若加計 FVOCI 股票資本利得
之調整後每股盈餘則年增 114%至 18.52 元，凱基預估 2026F 現金股利至少
7.5-8 元以上，現金股利殖利率 4.9%-5.3%。 
評論及分析 
前 10 月調整後獲利年增幅度擴大至 142%。富邦金 10 月稅後淨利虧損 7.5
億元，但實現 FVOCI 股票資本利得月增 52%至 142 億元，其中富壽稅後淨
利虧損 64.9 億元(FVTPL 股票及基金部位、FVTPL 債券評價分別虧損 30 億元
及 37 億元)，相較銀行獲利月增 14%。富邦金前 10 月稅後淨利年增 16%
至 1,264.7 億元，若加計 FVOCI 股票資本利得之調整後獲利則年增 142%至
2,634.3 億元，分別達凱基先前全年預估 78%及 90%。其中北富銀前 10 月
獲利年增 27%(淨利息收入及手續費收入各年增 29%及 36%)，加上證券及
產險獲利各年增 116%及 52%，抵銷富壽獲利年減 1.4%。 
3Q26 富邦金每股淨值季增 5.3 元，10 月壽險淨值比接近 16%。3Q26 富邦
金自結淨值 1.34 兆元，季增 737 億元，約當富邦金普通股每股淨值季增
5.8%(5.3 元)至 88.9 元，其中扣除 3Q26 保留盈餘影響數後，凱基推估其他權
益仍季增 122 億元，高於其他金控同業，意謂 3Q26 股票未實現損益增加及保
險負債公允價值下降，抵銷債券評價虧損。雖台股 10 月回檔 6%，富壽 10
月淨值比接近 16%，相較 9 月超過 16%，隱含其他權益下降幅度應有限。 
2026F 現金股利配發至少 7.5-8 元以上。富邦金股利政策現金股利配發率仍
以金控調整後盈餘 40%-50% 為基礎，但仍先考量子公司營運發展、資本適
足率水準。由於 1H26 富壽 TIS 資本適足率為 140%高於主要壽險同業，且
若未申請過渡措施之 TIS 達 110-120%，高於 100%法定標準，意謂富壽仍
有較高能力上繳獲利至金控，但仍需視主管機關規定及 2026F 年底 TIS 水
準。凱基上修 2026F 富邦金每股現金股利至少 7.5-8 元，目前預計股利配發
來源由非壽險子公司獲利上繳、壽險子公司獲利上繳、及金控銀行借款進行
支應，三者配置比例分別為 55%、20%與 25%。 
投資建議 
維持「增加持股」評等，目標價由 153 元上調至 175 元 (基於總和評量法模
型，約 2027F 股價淨值比 1.6 倍)，反映 2026F 現金股利至少 7.5-8 元，
2026F 配息能力優於主要壽險金控同業，預期 2027F 壽險核心獲利 CSM 攤
銷年增 10%、銀行淨利息及手續費收入維持中個位數以上成長。 
投資風險 
全球金融市場轉弱；人壽投資收益率不如預期；銀行信貸成本大增。 
增加持股‧維持

### Page 5

台灣 富邦金

十一月 16, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 5

圖 15: 公司概況  圖 16: 組織結構圖 
富邦金控成立於 2001 年 12月，旗下金融版圖橫跨保險、銀行、
證券、投信等業務，2025 年 9 月資產達 12.4 兆元台幣，在台灣
14 家金控中排名第 2。富邦金過去透過購併及內部成長，為資產
成長速度最快金控之一，旗下銀行事業陸續從 2002 購併臺北銀
行，2003 購併香港港基銀行(更名富邦銀行(香港))，2008 參股大
陸廈門銀行 20%股權，2014 年 1 月併購大陸華一銀行 80%股權
後，為兩岸三地唯一均擁有銀行子行之台資金融機構。富邦金
2009 年併購 ING 安泰人壽，富邦人壽目前為台灣保費收入第二
大之壽險業者，富邦產險為台灣最大的產險企業，市占率
24%。

### Page 7

台灣 富邦金

十一月 16, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 7

ESG 圖表定義 
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足
能源使用而生產的電力（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
富邦 2020 年人均用電量為 2133
度，較 2019 年上升 67 度，但與
2017、2018 年相比，已有逐年下滑
之趨勢。 
購買之再生能源 
Primary 再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質
能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
持續採購具節能、環保及綠建材標
章之產品，綠色採購金額逐年增
加。2020 年綠色採購金額新台幣
373 佰萬元，較 2019 年成長約
3.3%。 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 
(PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3) 
2020 年溫室氣體總排放量 44,825
噸，較 2019 年減少 8.4%，目標達
成率 444%。 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 
(PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總
量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
2020 年整體廢棄物重量 842,099 公
斤，較 2019 年減少 1.63%。 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
為配合防疫規劃，2020 年實施分區
辦公措施。在考量員工洗滌維持清
潔衛生下，導致 2020 整體用水量增
至 495,192 立方米，較 2019 年增加
1.84%；人均用水量 13.02 立方
米，較 2019 年增加 6.29%。 
環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處
理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗
員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
總離職率與自願離職率有逐年下降
之趨勢，2020 年總離職率為
9.5%，自願離職率為 8.3%。 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
2020 年女性主管(含中高階主管及基
層主管)比例為 55.2%，相較 2019
年之 54.8%，呈顯逐年成長。 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
2020年整體女性員工為 61.6%，較
2019 年的 61.1%持續成長。 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
2020年人均訓練時數為 35.9 小
時，人均訓練天數為 4.5 天。 
資料來源：Refinitiv；凱基

## Document 619: 凱基投顧_Takeaway_6223 旺矽_許芝瑄_20261116_C

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_Takeaway_6223 旺矽_許芝瑄_20261116_C.pdf

### Page 1

十一月 16, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   1

法說會重點摘要

旺矽 
(6223 TT, NT$4,305, 增加持股)

焦點內容 
1. 旺矽 3Q26 營收季增 12.8%。產品組合轉佳帶動毛利率季增 3.3 個
百分點至 61.7%，獲利季增 23.3%， EPS 為 19.18 元。展望
4Q26，ASIC 探針卡持續出貨，管理層預期營收季增 5-10%，毛利
率有望維持 3Q26 水準。 
2. 展望 2027 年，公司預估營收將年增至少 50%，毛利率維持 60%
以上，費用率則將降低至 23-25%。產能擴充方面，VPC 月產能將
增加至 2027 年底 250 萬針，MEMS 探針卡則增加至 600 萬針，且
2028 年仍將積極擴產。此外，旺矽與美系 CPU 大廠合作穩固，預
期將由筆電 CPU 切入，並逐步擴展至其他產品線。 
3. CPO 設備方面，Insertion 2 既有客戶認證持續進行中，若 12M26
結果順利即可能有量產訂單；另新增一國際大客戶合作機會，預期
1H27 將有明確結果。Insertion 3 已向 3 家客戶交付工程機，另有
3 家陸續交付，待 1H27 測試後將陸續轉為量產設備需求。然而設
備交期與驗收流程較長，公司預期 CPO 營收認列將大多落在 2028
年。 
結論 
凱基看好旺矽受惠高階測試需求強勁，加上 MEMS 探針卡產能於 2027
年大幅擴增，營運能見度已延伸至 2028 年。前次報告給予「增加持
股」投資評等，目標價 7,000 元，係基於 35 倍 2028 年每股盈餘預估
換算得出。 
3Q26 財報 
 營收：59.0 億元，季增 12.8%，年增 72.9% 
 毛利率：61.7%，季增 3.3ppts，年增 8.3ppts 
 3Q26 高毛利產品出貨增加，2Q26 毛利率較低屬季度現象 
 營業利益率：38.6%，季增 4.1ppts，年增 12.3ppts 
 業外收益：1.0 億元 
 獲利：18.8 億元，季增 23.3%，年增 114.4% 
 EPS：19.18 元 
 對比 2Q26 為 15.55 元，3Q25 為 9.30 元 
 非流動資產：169 億元，主因土地、廠房及設備增加

3Q26 營收分布 
 Probe card：72.7% 
 AI/HPC 約 60% 
 網通約 20%

 預期 4Q26 毛利率可望與 3Q26 相當

高於 2026 年 
 費用率：4Q26 預期將維持 25%下緣；2027 年預估為 23-25% 
 產能擴充 
 VPC 月產能 2026 年底為 200 萬針，2027 年將增加至 250 萬針 
 MEMS 探針卡月產能 2026 年底為 350 萬針，2027 年將增加至

 2028 年仍將積極擴產，目前擴產速度仍無法滿足客戶需求 
 CPC 稼動率 1-10M26 接近滿載；VPC 及 MEMS 探針卡全年稼動
率皆超過 100% 
 客戶進展 
 公司為多數客戶的主要供應商，展望 2027 年部分客戶市占率可
望提升 
 美系 CPU 客戶合作穩固，未來將由筆電 CPU 切入，並逐步擴展
至其他產品線 
 公司競爭優勢在於同時具備 CPC、VPC 及 MEMS 三種產品，並
提升自製探針及關鍵零組件能力；2027 年自製 PCB 投產後將大
幅降低對外部供應鏈的依賴 
 CPO 設備 
 Insertion 2 與 Insertion 3 並行推進，營收認列將大多落在 2028
年 
 Insertion 2 ：既有客戶認證持續進行中，若 12M26 結果順利即
可能有量產訂單，不需 pilot run ；另公司新增一國際大客戶，預
計 1H27 將有明確成果 
 Insertion 3 ：已向 3 家客戶交付工程機，另有 3 家陸續交付，客
戶涵蓋 Foundry、OSAT 及 Fabless 國際大廠，預計將於 1H27 測
試後轉為量產需求 
 公司認為 Insertion 2 不會被整合至 Insertion 3，主因客戶需釐清
wafer level 與 die level 的測試責任

Q&A 
 美系 CPU 大廠是否扶植公司成為主要供應商？切入順序？ 
 雙方合作意願強烈，客戶希望公司提供多元產品 
 目前將由筆電 CPU 切入，將逐步擴展至其他產品線 
 2028-29 年產能規劃是否延續近兩年擴產速度？同業擴產下如何看
待供需及競爭優勢？ 
 2028 年仍將積極擴產，目前擴產速度仍無法全面滿足客戶需求 
 CPO 設備量產訂單時程？Insertion 3 主要客戶及出貨時間？ 
 Insertion 2 通過驗證後即有量產訂單，不需 pilot run ；目前一家
客戶認證中，另新增一家國際大客戶合作機會，預計 1H27 有明
確進展 
 Insertion 3 客戶涵蓋 Foundry、OSAT 及 IC 設計國際一線大廠；
公司已向 3 家客戶交付工程機，另有 3 家陸續交付 
 市場傳言 Insertion 2 將整合至 Insertion 3？ 
 公司認為不符合實際客戶狀況 
 2027 年 CPO 設備的營收貢獻？ 
 1H27 可能取得部分量產訂單，但設備交期至少 6 個月，加上裝
機與驗收流程長，營收認列將落在 2028 年 
 2027 年營收年增 50%指引未計入積極計算的 CPO 營收貢獻 
 Insertion 2 與 Insertion 3 設備的單價及技術差異？ 
 定價皆為每台 US$1mn 
 Insertion 2 主要競爭者為 ficonTEC，公司為目前唯一通過跨認證
的廠商 
 Insertion 3 包含一家中國及一家台灣競業，各家設備負責不同測
試段 
 公司認為競業多為基礎方案，若未來世代精度要求提高，其產品
可能需要升級 
 3Q26 匯率對毛利率的影響？ 
 公司營收六到七成以美元計價，美元每升值 1 元影響毛利率約
0.6-0.7ppts

## Document 640: 博智(8155,Note)-CTBC261113

Source PDF: all report pdf/博智(8155,Note)-CTBC261113.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

2026 /11/13

1

前日收盤價                         2 5 5 . 00元

王翊安 
wade.wang@ctbcsis.com

PCB 
博智(8155 TT)           法說/座談摘要 
高階產品比重提升，越南廠接棒成長 
⚫ 3Q26毛利率升至 26.85%，高階產品比重提高。 
⚫ AI Server CPU板成長明顯，越南廠預計 2Q27量產。 
⚫ 太空資料中心為潛在新增題材。 
結論： 
3Q26 營收 16.81 億元(QoQ+5.5% ， YoY+38.6%) ， 毛 利 率
26.85%(QoQ+3.03ppts，YoY+6.21ppts)，EPS 4.49，營收符合預期，亦符
合公司先前年底前單月營收 5~6億元的指引。毛利率明顯提升，主因為
產品組合優化，高階產品出貨比重提高，且材料成本轉嫁順利。展望 2027
年，公司表示需求仍以 AI Server為主，目前以 ASIC Server的 CPU板成
長最明顯，IPC主要客戶研華 B/B ratio大於 1，越南廠 ACM預計 2Q27
量產。我們預期在 AI Server與 IPC需求延續下，若 ACM如期量產貢獻，
2027年營收獲利有望持續創高。本次法說亮點為太空資料中心，公司表
示其設計與地面資料中心無太大差異，公司有機會參與 AI 衛星相關產
品；我們認為若後續順利切入供應鏈，將為 ASIC客戶以外新增題材與
營運動能。我們正面看待博智後續營運發展。 
一、營運概況 
1. 公司員工人數 1,200人以上，產品為硬板 PCB，設備能力最大板
厚 8.0mm、層數 2~72L。台灣總部位於龍潭，新租廠房已於 2026
年投產；越南新廠 ACM位於河內，目前裝機中，預計 2Q27量
產。 
2. 主要股東為仁寶(2324)集團持股 29.42%、研華(2395)集團持股
5.70%，合計 35.12%。 
3. 1~3Q26產品組合以層別區分，2L~12L占 23%，14L~20L占 50%，
主要為 Server，22L~48+L占 27%，亦有 Server、IPC；以應用
別區分，Server占 82%、IC Tester占 9%、IPC占 7%、Networking
占 1%、Others占 1%。Server占比由先前約 90%下降，主因其
他類別增加，今年起 IC Tester自 IPC獨立列示。 
4. 技術方面，除 N+N對稱設計外，客戶開始採用 N+M不對稱設計。
不對稱結構容易板彎翹，須控制在一定程度內，同時還要進行
數千至數萬孔的背鑽，難度較高。公司以 Sintering技術將不同
板子堆疊，解決縱橫比過高的問題。 
5. 越南廠 ACM為公司與 Meiko合資，Meiko本身生產手機用 mSAP
產品。第一期廠房於 2026年 8月完成點交，9月設備進機，預
計 1Q27取得營運執照，2Q27正式量產。 
二、3Q26財報概況 
1. 3Q26營收 16.81億元(QoQ+5.5%，YoY+38.6%)。 
2. 毛利率 26.85%(QoQ+3.03ppts，YoY+6.21ppts)，大幅成長主因為
產品組合，下半年高階產品出貨比重較高；材料成本上漲部分，
客戶清楚上游狀況，轉嫁順利。

### Page 2

【中信投顧投資分析報告】

2026 /11/13

2

3. 營業利益 3.08 億元(QoQ+31.2%，YoY+117.1%)，營業利益率 18.31%(QoQ+3.59ppts，
YoY+6.62ppts)。 
4. 業外收益 0.16億元，稅前淨利 3.24億元(QoQ+33.6%，YoY+117.3%)。 
5. 歸屬母公司稅後淨利 2.78億元(QoQ+34.1%，YoY+120.5%)，EPS 4.49元。 
三、1~3Q26財報概況 
1. 1~3Q26營收 48.11億元(YoY+35.0%)。 
2. 毛利率 24.95%(YoY+10.53ppts)，營業利益 7.59億元，營業利益率 15.77%(YoY+10.04ppts)。 
3. 業外收益 0.37億元，稅前淨利 7.96億元，歸屬母公司稅後淨利 6.81億元(YoY+274.5%)，
EPS 11.15元。 
四、營運展望 
1. 目標市場依序為 Server、IPC、網通及 IC Tester。Server涵蓋 Intel、AMD平台的 General 
Server、ASIC Server(CPU板、I/O板、配卡)及 NVIDIA AI Server(配卡、CPU板)，其中
NVIDIA Vera CPU板有釋出給 ODM，公司配合 ODM生產。現行 Server產品層數 8~30L、
排版最大 22x27"、板厚 5.0mm、一次壓合、材料 M6~M7。 
2. IPC應用於工業機器設備、醫療、機器人等，層數 2~26L、排版最大 20x24"、板厚 3.0mm、
材料 M4~M6。IC Tester產品包含 Load Board、Burn-in Board及 SLT，其中 SLT打樣中，
層數 6~48L、排版最大 24x28"、板厚 6.4mm、材料 M4~M6。 
3. 未來兩到三年，Server層數將更高(>30L)，出現 18+18、20+20結構，排版最大 24x30"、
板厚 6.5mm，疊構走向 N+M或加上 HDI(HDI主要為 GPU或其他相關配卡)，材料升級
至 M8~M10。IPC無太大變化。IC Tester已看到 50L以上需求，排版最大 24x30"、板厚
10.0mm，疊構更複雜，為 N+N或 N+M，材料維持 M4~M6。 
4. 訂單能見度方面，Server為 6~8個月，ASIC客戶下單較長、傳統 Server較短；IPC為
6~12個月，主要客戶研華的採購反映狀況良好，因產品少量多樣，目前預估需求均已提
供；IC Tester材料用量較少，能見度 3~6個月。 
5. CCL供應商為台灣三家。供應商 A的 E-布交期 16~20週、K-布 8~12週，2027年將調度
其大陸廠支援，公司亦爭取增加配額，目前回應正面。供應商 B的 E-布交期 12~16週、
K-布 4~6週，交貨穩定、配額足夠。供應商 C的 E-布交期 8~12週、K-布 6~8週，博智
為其主要客戶，配額足夠。 
6. 客戶端目前很願意配合下長單並建立替代料，對板廠而言則需要更多時間完成替代料導
入。 
7. 展望 2027年，公司與客戶討論後認為 AI需求不可能消失，AI Server持續增加；IPC B/B
值大於 1；IC Tester機種與客戶數增加，越南廠開出後，台灣廠有更多空間承接。 
8. 展望 2028年，Server仍以 AI Server為主，IC Tester比例增加。低軌衛星方面，合資夥
伴 Meiko原已生產地面端用板，目前正推進衛星端用板；另有 AI衛星(衛星 AI Server)
需求，公司亦有機會參與。 
9. 台灣廠稼動率 90%以上，目前可挑選能創造較高毛利的產品組合。 
10. 公司表示與 Meiko合作順利，對方非常有誠信。近期許多 PCB同業赴泰國擴產，但
泰國目前面臨大量技術人員缺口，同業須爭搶有限資源；公司在越南已聘僱當地員工協
助裝機並委託代訓，擴產進度可望優於同業。 
11. 台灣目前建廠速度緩慢，因台積電吸走資源，公司評估在台灣新建廠房從設計到完工
約需三年；ACM可在 9個月內完成一座廠房，主因 Meiko支援。越南廠 4Q26已開始內
裝，同步進行設備進機，進度符合預期。 
12. 目前 PCB與 CCL需求高於供給，非短期可解決。公司具備 SI能力，轉換板材時可做

2026 /11/13

3 
得較仔細，雖然 lead time有所延長，但有 2nd source選項為公司優勢，目前訂單能見度
長。 
13. 目前產業瓶頸在 HBM，整體產業需求強勁，但供應鏈短時間內無法紓解。 
14. 董事長個人看法：美國期中選舉後，資料中心因土地與電力資源問題，設置管控可能
趨嚴，建置速度或稍微減緩，但整體需求仍在，產業發展可望更健康、走得更久。

五、Q&A 
Q1：ASIC是否有新客戶？ 
A1：公司已有 ASIC客戶，新客戶部分主要受限於產能不足，待越南廠開出後即可承接。 
Q2：是否有承接 AWS加速卡？ 
A2：目前沒有。加速卡需用到六階 HDI，壓合、電鍍須 6~7次，一般板子僅 2~3次，代表壓合
與電鍍產線要倍增。公司在 HDI、sequential、厚板、背鑽等技術上都沒有問題，但工廠已滿載、
沒有新廠可擴充，故目前接單以能創造最大產值為考量。 
Q3：越南廠的產品與技術規劃？ 
A3：前年底規劃時以 18L板為主，當時預估 N+N、N+M、24x30"大板、5mm厚板等需求合計
不到 10%，留在台灣生產即可。過去一年 N+N、N+M成為 ASIC主板主流，不只用於加速卡，
大板需求也出現，因此重新評估越南廠產能配置。目前規劃台灣能做的越南廠也能做，除 IC測
試板不做外，其他產品均可生產。 
Q4：越南廠損平時程？ 
A4：不便透露。第二個廠區正在興建，第一個廠區進行內裝與設備進機，設備從進機到運作約
需半年。 
Q5：4Q26及 2027年毛利率展望？ 
A5：希望更高。AI Server設計一年前為單純一次壓合，現在出現 N+M壓合；Intel General Server
板約 18L，AI Server層數更高，毛利會更好，難度越高的板子，毛利不應與一般板相同。 
Q6：4Q26及 2027年營運展望？ 
A6：目前 PCB技術變化太快，越南廠原本設定的產品層級近兩個月已有修改；2027年只要產
能到位，訂單不會有問題。 
Q7：衛星板規模及預計市占？ 
A7：Meiko本身即為 SpaceX供應商，雙方持續討論衛星市場，越南廠有機會承接。 
Q8：太空資料中心的看法？ 
A8：與地面資料中心沒有太大不同，僅太空環境有特殊條件，若有差異也是材質改變，設計上
與地面相同。 
Q9：CCL與 PCB交期變化？ 
A9：PCB交期與 CCL交期相近；若為 sequential、HDI產品，製程時間約為 1.4~1.5倍。

### Page 4

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

2026 /11/13

4 
Q10：IC Tester客戶認證進度？ 
A10：目前多家客戶都在進行中。IC Tester較少有正式認證過程，客戶多直接提供產品詢問能否
製作。 
Q11：N+M的應用？ 
A11：應用於 Server主板、ASIC CPU板、Switch板，主要目的是降低縱橫比，40、50層以上
因鑽針長度有限，須以此方式降低縱橫比。一般 AI Server縱橫比 10以上即不易製作，IC Tester
縱橫比容易達 20~30倍，且沒有明確規格可參照，板廠須具備自有製程設計能力。 
Q12：良率表現？ 
A12：不會像一次壓合那麼好。 
Q13：稼動率？ 
A13：台灣廠 90%以上。 
Q14：是否有外包？ 
A14：特定製程塞車時會外包給台灣其他廠商，但僅為應急方法，長期外包不如直接買設備自己
做。 
Q15：機器人產品類型？ 
A15：機器人屬於 IPC，包含人形與非人形，目前非人形做得較多，客戶為研華，研華亦有人形
機器人產品。醫療、AMR也屬 IPC，IPC板並不複雜。 
Q16：目前哪些產品成長明顯？ 
A16：ASIC的 CPU板成長最明顯。傳統 Server目前以 AMD為主，Intel平台有遞延。 
Q17：IPC是否有更多需求？ 
A17：需求較去年增加，產品包含醫療、機器人、Edge AI設備等應用，板子不大，但信賴度要
求高、種類多。

## Document 678: 家計のおカネは株式から国債へ 日銀の利上げの影響

Source PDF: all report pdf/家計のおカネは株式から国債へ 日銀の利上げの影響.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026年 11月 18日(水)

1/2 
家計のおカネは株式から国債へ 日銀の利上げの影響    
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・昨日の当レポート「中央銀行と闘うな 利上げ局面で株式は弱い 」では、最近のＡＩ・半導
体株を中心とする世界的な株価調整は、Ｆｅｄを中心とする主要中銀の利上げが重要な理由
であると述べた。事実、米10月ＣＰＩが発表される直前にウォラー理事が50bpの利上げに言
及し、Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測が高まったことで、株式に対する逆風は強くなった。その点、Ｆ
ｅｄの合意形成に影響を与えることも多いウィリアムズ・ＮＹ連銀総裁が11月17日に50bpの
利上げに否定的な見解を示したことは安心材料である。同氏は「ここ数カ月の政策運営はう
まく機能していると思うが、インフレ率を適時、かつ持続的に２％に戻すためにまだやるべ
き仕事は残っている」「このところの経済指標で米経済が底堅さを維持し⁠ていることが明確
に示されており、労働市場も堅調だが、インフレは望ましい方向に向かっていない」としつ
つも「金融政策による劇的な対応が必要な状況ではない」との見解を示した。ここでいう
「やるべき仕事」は12月ＦＯＭＣにおける25bpの追加利上げ、「劇的な対応」は50bp利上げ
を指していると考えられる。11月入り後、堅調な雇用統計と強いＣＰＩが重なったことで、
追加利上げが正当化された一方、賃金上昇率の鈍化や住宅市況の軟化といった状況が継続
し、インフレ加速の証左に乏しいなか、やはり50bpの利上げは唐突な印象が拭えない。Ａ
Ｉ・半導体株の急落によって金融環境が引き締まったことも50bp利上げの必要性を減じたと
考えられる。 
・日経平均株価は先行き 12ヶ月 72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き 12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を 12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに 2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利（誘導目標上限）を、12月に4.25％へ引き上げるだろう

### Page 2

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
2/2
・この間、日銀の利上げ継続も株価下押し要因として効いている。金融環境は引き続き緩和的
であるが、日銀が言うとおり緩和度合いが低下しているのは事実であり、これも日本株の頭
を抑えていると考えられる。身近な例として「個人向け国債」の販売好調があろう。財務省
によれば、10月募集分（11月発行）は2.03兆円に達したという。販売全体の65％を占めた固
定５年の利率は税引き前2.39％であり、この絶対水準に惹かれた家計が多かったと推察され
る。2024年３月にマイナス金利を解除して以降、しばらくの間は金利の絶対水準が「雀の
涙」程度であり、個人向け国債の販売額に大きな変化はなかった。しかしながら、政策金利
が2026年６月に１％となった後、９月には1.25％となり、家計が金利を実感できるようにな

ったことで、非線形な影響がみられつつある。個人向け国債の販売増加それ自体は、インフ
レ下における個人の資産形成において重要な役割を果たすものであり歓迎すべきであろう。
一方、株式市場に対してはネガティブな面がある。というのも、かつて株価の急落する局面
では個人投資家による押し目買いが相場を支える構図が一般的であったが、そうした構図が
変化した可能性があるためだ。象徴的だったのは日経平均株価が前週末比5855円（9.3％）も
下落した11月２～６日の投資部門別売買動向。東証・名証２市場の現物取引で個人投資家は
4,802億円の売り越しを記録した（売り越しは３週連続）。10月中旬以降の株価下落局面で、
家計はリスクをとって株式の下値を拾うよりも、安全に利回りを取れる個人向け国債を選好
した格好となる。従来、株式相場に流入してきたおカネが（個人向け）国債へ流れたこと
で、相場を下支えする力が弱くなった印象である。日銀の利上げが株式市場に染み渡ってい
ることを物語る。
